### Core: JWT validation and config (epic)

- Status: Ready · Milestone: Level 1 · Effort: M · Component: core · Level: Some(1)

<!-- megabase-id: epic:core:jwt:1 -->

Shared `megabase-core` types so Auth and REST can enforce the same JWT and role claims as GoTrue / PostgREST.

## Scope

**In:** parse and verify the HS256 tokens the demo `JWT_SECRET` issues; `role`, `sub`, `exp`; config from env. **Out:** asymmetric keys (later), JWKS rotation.

## Acceptance

- Tokens the reference Auth issues are accepted.
- Forged / expired / `alg=none` tokens are rejected the same way.
- Judge cases `auth.user.get` and REST RLS cases pass once those units exist.
- Blocks Auth/database and REST/resources.

**Effort:** M. **Milestone:** Level 1. **Labels:** `component:core`, `level:1`, `type:infra`.

### Judge: database side-effect checks (epic)

- Status: Backlog · Milestone: Level 1 · Effort: L · Component: judge · Level: Some(1)

<!-- megabase-id: epic:judge:side-effects:1 -->

After mutating cases, snapshot `auth.users` and the public fixtures on both databases and compare. HTTP equality is not enough for sign-up.

**In:** harness support + Auth sign-up / REST insert cases. **Out:** Storage objects (Level 2).

On a `review/*` branch only ([ADR 0003](docs/adr/0003-protected-paths.md)).

**Effort:** L. **Milestone:** Level 1. **Labels:** `component:judge`, `level:1`, `type:infra`.

### Website (epic)

- Status: Backlog · Milestone: — · Effort: XL · Component: studio · Level: None

<!-- megabase-id: epic:website:site:later -->

Public website for the experiment. **Not a compatibility level.** Status embeds `coverage/treemap.svg` (same file as the README). Follows the design-first rule:

1. Design in [Kite: Megabase identity](https://kite.new/p/megabase-identity).
2. LLM committee review (several external models).
3. Apply corrections in Kite.
4. Implement in this repository so assets match Kite.

## Scope

**In:** marketing/docs site, social images, diagrams. **Out:** Studio rewrite, product API.

Child issues below must be done **in order**. Do not implement before the committee signs off.

**Effort:** XL. **Labels:** `type:feature`. No level milestone.
